| KA3-window | Legacy has no Kanji timer. Aegisub's (DialogKanjiTimer, dialog_kara_timing_copy.cpp) is a modal dialog: the two styles are chosen and Start pressed before anything shows, and a wrong choice is a message box ("Select source and destination styles first.", "The source and destination styles must be different."). | Subtitles > Kanji timer… (after Check karaoke) and the rewrite's own Global command `GLOBAL_KANJI_TIMER` (5174, no key by default) open a modeless, resizable window of its own on DG1's frame. It guesses the styles on opening (the style with the most `\k` Lines for the romaji; then a style named with "kanji" or "jp", else the next style with Lines, for the kanji) and shows the pairs at once; there is no Start. The same style twice, or a romaji style without `\k` Lines, is said in the window, not in a message box. The "Suggest groupings" choice is kept in the profile (`audio.kanjiTimerSuggest`, Aegisub's Tool/Kanji Timer/Interpolation, on by default). | Card KA3 [#278](https://github.com/altqx/hikari/issues/278) (wave 6b, [mockup](https://github.com/altqx/hikari/issues/278) in the four themes, owner-approved 2026-10-23). Validation: `shell_tests` kanjiTimerWindowTimesTheKanjiLines, `hotkeys_tests`. |
| KA3-whole-song | Aegisub shows one source and one destination Line at a time, walking forward from the top; nothing shows which Lines are done or still to do. | The window lists every pair of the song on the left (number, kanji and romaji text, and its state: Suggested, Check, To do, Done, Applied, Changed, No kanji line, No romaji line; the current pair shows how many of its groups are linked). A click opens any pair; Skip romaji line and Skip kanji line leave a Line out and pair the rest again. | Card KA3 [#278](https://github.com/altqx/hikari/issues/278) (wave 6b, [mockup](https://github.com/altqx/hikari/issues/278) in the four themes, owner-approved 2026-10-23). Validation: `kanji_timer_tests` PairsByTimeWithLinesLeftAlone; `shell_tests` kanjiTimerWindowTimesTheKanjiLines. |
| KA3-pair-by-time | Aegisub pairs the n-th source-style Line with the n-th destination-style Line in file order (FindNextStyleMatch), so one Line too many on either side shifts every pair after it until skipped by hand. | Lines pair by time: in start order, each romaji Line with the first kanji Line after the previous pair that overlaps it in time; Lines that overlap none are listed alone. A Line that only touches its partner, most of it outside it, goes alone when the next Line overlaps that partner more (a stray Line before the real one); a Line lying within its partner always keeps it. When no romaji Line overlaps any kanji Line (a file timed apart), they pair by order as in Aegisub. Templater fx Lines (Effect `fx`) are never read. | Card KA3 [#278](https://github.com/altqx/hikari/issues/278) (wave 6b, [mockup](https://github.com/altqx/hikari/issues/278) in the four themes, owner-approved 2026-10-23). Validation: `kanji_timer_tests` PairsByTimeWithLinesLeftAlone, ALineWithinItsPartnerStaysPaired, PairsByOrderWithoutAnyOverlap. |
| KA3-suggest-whole-line | Aegisub's interpolation ("Attempt to interpolate grouping", AutoMatchKaraoke) proposes only the next group, after each link. | The same matcher runs group after group to the end of the Line, so the whole suggestion shows dashed after the current group; Ctrl+Enter accepts the Line with it, and "Accept all suggestions" marks done every pair whose suggestion is clean (no blank group, no group that ran out of kanji, no kanji left over). A suggestion that is not clean shows Check. | Card KA3 [#278](https://github.com/altqx/hikari/issues/278) (wave 6b, [mockup](https://github.com/altqx/hikari/issues/278) in the four themes, owner-approved 2026-10-23). Validation: `kanji_timer_tests` SuggestsTheWholeLineAsAegisubWould, RunningOutOfKanjiIsACheck, AFirstGroupThatRanOutIsACheck, ALineBreakInASyllableIsASpace, ABlankSyllableWithinAReadingIsInItsGroup, AcceptLineTakesTheSuggestionAndUndoRedo, AcceptAllTakesTheCleanSuggestionsAndApplyMarksThem. |
| KA3-apply | Aegisub writes the accepted Lines when the dialog closes, by Close or Esc, as one "kanji timing" commit; there is no way to close without them. | Apply writes the done pairs as one "Kanji timing" undo step and the window stays open. Closing (Close, Esc, the title bar) with done pairs not yet applied asks "Apply the kanji timing of N lines?" with Apply, Discard and Cancel. Window-level Ctrl+Z and Ctrl+Y undo and redo the window's own steps before Apply. KA3-close-review: a Document's done pairs not applied (its own, or kept while another tab was the target) are unsaved work of that Document, so closing its tab, Close all tabs or quitting lists it in the close review even when the Document is unchanged; Save writes them as the "Kanji timing" step and then saves, Don't save drops them. | Card KA3 [#278](https://github.com/altqx/hikari/issues/278) (wave 6b, [mockup](https://github.com/altqx/hikari/issues/278) in the four themes, owner-approved 2026-10-23). Validation: `shell_tests` kanjiTimerWindowTimesTheKanjiLines; `kanji_timer_tests` AcceptLineTakesTheSuggestionAndUndoRedo, UndoKeepsWhatEachStepChanged; `close_review_tests` kanjiTimerPairsNotAppliedAreReviewed. |
| KA3-keep-tags | Aegisub matches against the destination Line's stripped text (GetStrippedText) and writes `{\kN}` before each group, so the kanji Line loses every override tag it had, and a `\kf` or `\ko` romaji Line gives plain `\k`. | Only the kanji Line's karaoke tags are replaced; its other tags and blocks stay where they were, a group's tag going into the Line's own block just before it (`{\i1}君の` becomes `{\i1\k70}君{\k30}の`). Each group's tag takes the letters of its first syllable's (`\kf` stays `\kf`). | Card KA3 [#278](https://github.com/altqx/hikari/issues/278) (wave 6b, [mockup](https://github.com/altqx/hikari/issues/278) in the four themes, owner-approved 2026-10-23). Validation: `kanji_timer_tests` KanjiTextDropsItsKaraokeTagsOnly, OutputKeepsTheTagLettersAndTheLinesOwnTags. |
| KA3-leftover-kanji | In Aegisub a Line is accepted once every source syllable is grouped; destination characters no group took are left out of the output (GetOutputLine writes the matched groups only). | Kanji left after the last group join that group, so the kanji Line keeps all its text; such a pair's suggestion is not clean, so it shows Check. | Card KA3 [#278](https://github.com/altqx/hikari/issues/278) (wave 6b, [mockup](https://github.com/altqx/hikari/issues/278) in the four themes, owner-approved 2026-10-23). Validation: `kanji_timer_tests` LeftoverKanjiJoinTheLastGroup, EveryOutputAddsUpToTheRomaji. |
| KA3-go-back | Aegisub's Go back drops the last accepted Line and steps both styles back one Line each, whatever was skipped, so after a skip the pair it reopens is not the one done before. | Previous goes to the previous pair with both Lines, keeping its grouping; a click on a linked group reopens it (that group and the ones after it are unlinked), and a click on a kanji or syllable ends the current group there. | Card KA3 [#278](https://github.com/altqx/hikari/issues/278) (wave 6b, [mockup](https://github.com/altqx/hikari/issues/278) in the four themes, owner-approved 2026-10-23). Validation: `kanji_timer_tests` ClicksEndTheGroupOrReopenALinkedOne, KeysLinkUnlinkAndAccept. |
| KA8-multi-highlight | Aegisub's Kanji Timer only folds: a group of several source syllables becomes one `\k` whose time is their sum (GetOutputLine). | A Kanji timer option, "Multi-highlight (#)" (`audio.kanjiTimerMulti`, off by default, so folding stays the default), writes each group of more than one syllable with its text on the first syllable's tag and each further syllable as a `#` syllable after the group's characters, with its own time and its own `\k` letters (k, kf, ko or K, as the romaji wrote them). That is the karaskel multi-highlight form that kara-templater's `multi` modifier reads, one highlight per sung syllable. The total time is unchanged. A group of one syllable, a blank group and the Line's own tags are written as KA3 writes them, and kanji left after the last group stay in it, before its `#` syllables. A kanji Line that already has `#` syllables (`#` or `＃` as a whole syllable after the first tag) loses them from its kanji, and when its tags take every romaji syllable its pair opens with those groups linked. The audio box labels a `#` syllable `#`, as written, and the karaoke checks read it as one more syllable. | Card KA8 [#354](https://github.com/altqx/hikari/issues/354) (owner request 2026-10-26, wave 6b). Validation: `kanji_timer_tests` MultiHighlightWritesAHashForEachFurtherSyllable, MultiHighlightKeepsEachSyllablesLettersAndTheLinesTags, MultiHighlightLeavesSingleBlankAndLeftoverAsTheyWere, HashSyllablesReadBackAsTheirGroups, ALineOfManyHashSyllablesReadsQuickly, ALineWithHashSyllablesOpensWithItsGroupsLinked, MultiHighlightIsAppliedAndStaysApplied, EveryOutputAddsUpToTheRomaji; `karaoke_templater_tests` KanjiTimerHashSyllablesHighlightOncePerSyllable; `karaoke_checks_tests` MultiHighlightHashSyllablesAreSyllables; `audio_karaoke_tests` MultiHighlightHashSyllablesAreLabelledAsWritten; `shell_tests` kanjiTimerWritesMultiHighlightSyllables; `close_review_tests` kanjiTimerPairsKeptAreSavedWithMultiHighlightAsItIsNow. |
| KA8-join-multi-highlight | The audio box's Join puts the two syllables' texts together (Karaoke::Join), so joining a syllable with the `#` syllable after it gives `撃#`, and the templater shows that `#` as a character. | A joined syllable is a `#` syllable only when both halves were. Otherwise the `#` is dropped and the highlight folds back into the other syllable: `撃` + `#` gives `撃`, `#` + `腐` gives `腐`, `#` + `#` gives `#`, and the `#` syllable's own blocks stay. A `#` among other text is no `#` syllable and joins as before. Times are as Join already makes them. | Card KA8 [#354](https://github.com/altqx/hikari/issues/354), owner 2026-10-27, option 1 ([issuecomment-6436216989](https://github.com/altqx/hikari/issues/354#issuecomment-6436216989)). Validation: `audio_karaoke_tests` JoinWithAHashSyllableFoldsItsHighlightBack. |
| SV1-screen-size-subtitles | Legacy drew the subtitles at the video's encoded size (SubtitlesLibass at the frame's size) and scaled the result with the picture, so they look soft on a large or high-DPI screen, and in the Style manager's and the font lists' previews too. | Settings > Video > "Preview subtitles at" (profile setting `video.previewSubtitlesAt`): "Video resolution (as encoded)", the default, draws as before. "Screen size (sharp)" has libass draw at the picture's size on screen in device pixels (`ass_set_frame_size`), at most 7680×4320. The storage size stays the video's (`ass_set_storage_size`), so the layout, `\blur` and unscaled borders match Video resolution. It applies to the Video panel and full screen (the Template editor's preview too, which the video draws), the Style manager's preview, and the samples in the font picker and the Fonts window, which draw again when the setting or their screen's scale changes. Playing, the next frame is drawn at a new size; paused, the shown frame is drawn again. Frames copied or saved (Automation's `get_frame`, snapshots) stay at the video's size. A CSRI filter draws at the video's size: with one chosen the row is turned off with "Video resolution only with this filter." | Owner, card [#365](https://github.com/altqx/hikari/issues/365) (SV1): the default stays Video resolution; "lgtm" on the mockup ([comment](https://github.com/altqx/hikari/issues/365#issuecomment-6434601729)). Validation: `libass_renderer_tests` AScreenSizeDrawsTheVideoSizesLayoutWithMorePixels, TheSelectionDrawsAtTheScreenSize; `playback_pipeline_tests` OverlaysAreDrawnAtTheScreenSize, OverlaySize.FollowsTheScreenUpToEightK; `video_session_tests` AScreenSizeDrawsTheShownFramesOverlayAgain; `presenter_tests` anOverlayAtScreenSizeIsPlacedOnTheFrame; `options_dialog_tests` BindsTheLegacyControlsInOrder; `shell_tests` settingsPreviewSubtitlesAtFollowsTheFilter; crops at 100 % and 200 % and the heavy-subtitle bench on the card. |
| SV1-hidpi-tool-sizes | Legacy was per-monitor DPI aware and drew the visual tools in device pixels at fixed sizes. At 200 % its handles, arrows, crosses, Rotate Z's ring, dashes and grab distances were half as large on screen and harder to grab. | The tools keep their geometry in device pixels, so positions are as precise as before. Legacy's sizes are multiplied by the screen's device pixel ratio, which keeps them in logical pixels. This covers the handles (squares, circles, the vector points), crosses, arrows, Rotate Z's ring and band, Rotate XY's grab, dashes, and the drag labels' offsets. It also covers every grab distance: the handles, Scale's arrows, the rectangle clip's and Drawing's edges, Shifter's points, Perspective's corners and the zoom frame's edges. Line widths are logical pixels, as the labels already were. At 100 % nothing changes. | Owner "lgtm" on the recommendation in card [#365](https://github.com/altqx/hikari/issues/365) ([comment](https://github.com/altqx/hikari/issues/365#issuecomment-6434601729)). Validation: `visual_position_tests` VisualPositionHidpi.HandlesKeepTheirLogicalSize; the capture replays at 100 % unchanged; crops at 200 % on the card. |
| DC1-depctrl-release | The legacy package ships DependencyControl 0.6.4's core (its updater, feeds, config, Toolbox 0.1.3) beside modules from the 0.9 development tree (`dkjson`, `MoonCats`, `AegisubShims`, about 40 `DependencyControl/*` files). 0.6.4 cannot expand the format-0.4.0 feed those modules name, so Install Script fails ("attempt to index a nil value") and the Toolbox often fails to load; its first-start self-update downloads 0.8.1 into the user folder (#361). | Ship one DependencyControl release, v0.9.1 (`c4101aeed1a9`): its modules, Toolbox, `dkjson`, `MoonCats` and `AegisubShims`, with five fixes marked `HikariSub (DC1)` (the Toolbox releases the updater lock after Install and Update Script, so other scripts' updates are not blocked while its helper lives; its Global Configuration log level starts at 2, as 0 and 1 make every ordinary message raise; its Package Source Configuration writes into the reloaded config, so a saved source is kept; a config table referenced twice is saved twice, not emptied; `DependencyControl.ConfigHandler` is `ConfigView`, the class of `getConfigHandler`'s result, so lyger's preset shortcuts keep their tags). Its own Downloader uses the system libcurl on Linux and WinInet on Windows; BadMutex, PreciseTimer and DownloadManager stay shipped for the scripts that require them. | User, 2026-10-27 on #361: "Fix it and update it to latest". Validation: `lua_helper_tests` DependencyControlInstallsAndUpdatesFromAFeed (install and update against a loopback feed), DependencyControlConfigHandlerIsTheConfigViewsClass, BundledScriptsLoadUnchanged (the Toolbox registers its macros); `automation_thirdparty_tests`; real-network installs on Linux and the Windows VM (#361). |
| DC1-moonscript | The legacy package ships MoonScript 0.3.0, which miscompiles a `return` inside a loop that `continue`s; DependencyControl 0.9's Toolbox fails to compile with it ("'until' expected"). | Ship MoonScript 0.5.0, the version Aegisub moved to in `3c5b61429a49` and arch1t3cht's Aegisub ships. Not 0.7.0 (TypesettingTools/Aegisub since August 2026): it rejects the bundled Aegisub-Motion `TrimHandler` (`"#{(.-)}"`, fixed upstream only in Aegisub-Motion 3675284c138c). | Same answer (the Toolbox of the latest release needs it). Validation: every bundled `.moon` and the third-party corpus compile with 0.5.0; `lua_helper_tests` BundledScriptsLoadUnchanged; `automation_thirdparty_tests`. |
| DC1-linux-ca-bundle | The Linux packages' DownloadManager links the curl built on the AlmaLinux floor, which keeps `/etc/pki/tls/certs/ca-bundle.crt`; on Arch, Debian and Ubuntu every DownloadManager download fails ("error adding trust anchors"). | On Linux, DownloadManager uses `SSL_CERT_FILE`, else the first CA bundle found at the usual distribution paths (Debian/Arch, Fedora/RHEL, openSUSE, the p11-kit extract, `/etc/ssl/cert.pem`). Windows is unchanged. | Same answer (a cause found in the #361 reproduction). Validation: the CI AppImage on Arch downloads through DownloadManager (#361 evidence). |
| DC1-depctrl-fresh-config | With DependencyControl 0.6.4, a script config view's `load` returns false when its file or section does not exist yet, and scripts written against it seed defaults on that branch: lyger's FbfTransform adds its "All" preset and GradientEverything its "Horizontal all" preset on first start. | DependencyControl 0.9.1's `load` returns true for a fresh config, so on a profile without their config those two scripts start without that example preset (they still load, with Default and [Last Settings]); a config carried over from legacy keeps its presets. The upstream behaviour is kept (DC1-depctrl-release). | Follows from DC1-depctrl-release (user, 2026-10-27, #361); upstream has no later release or report. Validation: `automation_thirdparty_tests` ScriptsLoadAsAutoloadDoes pins both scripts' macros without the example preset. |
